Today the bytes happen to match (verified: `diff` of both copies of scripts/runner-wrapper/run-ephemeral.sh → identical), so nothing is actively broken; the exposure is that any wrapper-level change (for example exporting OMNISIGHT_RUNNER_LEARNED_ITEMS into the runner env, the fix for finding 1) would be merged-but-dead with a green sync unit reporting success. The sweep flagged the sora-bridge twin and stopped there.

### [MEDIUM] /api/v1/learned-items/block takes the tenant from the caller and never checks it against the authenticated principal

- **category:** security  ·  **EVIDENCE**  ·  **silent:** True
- **evidence:** backend/routers/learned_items.py:32-41 — `async def learned_items_block(tenant: str | None = None, ..., user: _au.User = Depends(_au.current_user))` then `get_learned_items_block(tenant_id=(tenant or None), ...)` with no membership check. backend/learned_item_loader.py:177-186 builds `scope_keys = ["global:-", f"tenant:{tenant_id}"]` directly from that value. The runner calls it as `/api/v1/learned-items/block?tenant=omnisight-self` (auto-runner-jira.py:1094).
- **expected vs actual:** Expected: a memory read is scoped to the caller's tenant, as the U6 memory-scope machinery does elsewhere (u6_memory_scope / _scope_for). Actual: the scope key is caller-supplied and unvalidated.
- **impact:** Latent cross-tenant memory disclosure: any principal holding a valid bearer (including the shared runner API token) can request another tenant's published learned-item cards. Not exploitable in prod today — the leg-2 store is empty and the read flag is unset — but it becomes live the moment leg-2 is activated. The kernel dimension audited write/side-effect authorization; nobody audited read scoping on the memory-serving endpoints.

### [INFO] Correction to the sweep's shared premise: the runner fleet is not idle-dead, and the log the sweep read is not the log the wrapper writes

- **category:** mismatch-with-intent  ·  **EVIDENCE**  ·  **silent:** True
- **evidence:** scripts/runner-wrapper/run-ephemeral.sh:35 sets `LOG="/tmp/runner-$INSTANCE.log"`. /tmp/runner-claude-1.log is 128 MB, last line `=== 23:34:38 claude-1 cycle #58148 end rc=0 ===` written today; `systemctl --user status runner-claude@1` shows a live `git clone` child mid-flight. Meanwhile /home/user/work/sora/logs/runner/claude-1.systemd.log (the path the axis-liveness auditor cited) was last written 2026-07-20 13:36 and is 1,080 bytes. The real cause of the quiet: `grep -a -c 'no candidate passed pre-pickup checks' /tmp/runner-claude-1.log` → 93,866 of 94,302 cycles.
- **expected vs actual:** The sweep reported 'the runner fleet went quiet on 2026-07-20 (no log file written since 13:36, no ticket pickups)' and both the axis-liveness and anti-hollow dimensions disclaimed it as out of scope. Actual: the fleet has been cycling continuously every ~40 s; what stopped is ticket *pickup* — ~99.5% of cycles pass the pre-pickup gate with no candidate.
- **impact:** The upstream cause of the empty causal axis (runner_incidents max created_at = 2026-07-20 05:40) is a pickup-gate/fuel problem, not a dead fleet — a materially different remediation. Two dimensions each assumed the other owned it and neither checked the live process, which is why the wrong log path went uncorrected. Secondary exposure: the only durable runner history lives in /tmp (gemini-1 1.38 GB, grok-1 1.37 GB), outside omnisight-log-retention's scope and lost on reboot.

## hollow-hunter: paths in the U6 / 3D memory stack that report SUCCESS while delivering NOTHING

**Auditor summary:** The Phase R/S repair moved the hollowness up a layer: the data paths mostly work now, but the anti-hollow detection layer built to guard them is itself hollow — Prometheus has zero alertmanagers, the "fail-closed" staging-gate project-state probe has never executed once in 11,222 canary runs (all green), and the one alert meant to catch "the memory injection stopped" is broken in two independent ways. I proved this empirically: on 2026-07-27, while the project-state endpoint served exactly zero requests for 9 days, all four project-state alert expressions evaluated to healthy/silent.

**Checks performed:**
- Read backend/agents/project_state_aggregator.py (1049 lines) in full — all three axis fetchers, classifier, trace surface
- Read backend/api/project_state.py, backend/agents/project_state_cache.py, backend/agents/agent_feature_flags.py
- Read backend/agents/action_guard.py (mode matrix, guard_tool_dispatch stages), backend/agents/authorization_kernel.py head
- Read backend/routers/learned_items.py, backend/learned_item_loader.py head, backend/agents/u6_memory_scheduler.py, backend/agents/incident_recorder.py, backend/agents/failure_graph.py PostgresIncidentSource
- Read auto-runner-jira.py project-state + learned-items injection block (lines 1040-1300)
- curl http://localhost:9090/api/v1/alertmanagers -> activeAlertmanagers: []
- curl http://localhost:9090/api/v1/rules and /api/v1/alerts (live alert states + activeAt)
- PromQL queries: omnisight_project_state_axis_total, ..._structural_half_total, max_over_time over 60d, query_range 30d step 1d
- PromQL negative-control battery: count() on 4 metric names + absent(rate(...)) on a real vs a nonexistent metric
- PromQL evaluation of ProjectStateNoTraffic and ProjectStateStructuralHollow exprs at time=2026-07-27T12:00Z (the dead period)
- docker inspect omnisight-productizer-prometheus-1 mounts; stat inode+mtime of prometheus.yml on host vs inside container
- docker exec omnisight-productizer-backend-a-1 env (names only) — checked for OMNISIGHT_ACTION_GUARD_MODE, U6/L2/L3/learned-item flags
- curl http://localhost:8000/api/v1/metrics — full exposition, 191 omnisight_ series
- psql SELECTs on omnisight-pg-primary: chat_messages, chat_session_summaries, l3_facts/approvals/eval_runs, claude_memory_{versions,state,exports}, learned_item_{versions,snapshots,citations,utility}, runner_incidents (counts, per-day, per-failure_class, MEMORY_RECALL_AUDIT)
- systemctl --user is-active/is-enabled/list-timers --all; show -p LastTriggerUSec on cognee timers
- Diffed deploy/systemd/*.{service,timer} against ~/.config/systemd/user/ to find shipped-but-never-installed units
- Read ~/.config/systemd/user/staging-gate-canary.service + runner-claude@.service + dropins; confirmed staging-gate.env absent
- grep -c 'OP-2558|project_state_probe' /home/user/sora-bridge/backend/agents/staging_gate.py -> 0; git rev-list --count HEAD..origin/develop -> 247
- Scanned all 11,222 lines of /home/user/work/sora/logs/release-milestone/canary-status.jsonl for the string 'project_state'
- Ran backend.agents.lesson_retrieval.retrieve_lessons locally against docs/sop/lessons with a nonsense ticket key to test BM25 hit floor
- Checked ~/.local/state/omnisight-alerts/{spool.jsonl,run.log} to establish which alert channel actually delivers
- Checked /var/omnisight/memory tree and the memory_tool_monitor DEFAULT_METRICS path

**Could not check:**
- Whether Grafana dashboards surface the u6_l2/l3 gauges to a human who actually looks — I did not open Grafana.
- Staging (omnisight-staging-*) leg-2 state; every DB/metric check above was against prod (omnisight-pg-primary / backend-a).
- Whether backend-b (port 8001) has ever served a project-state request — no series exist for that instance, but I could not distinguish 'never routed there' from 'never exported'.
- Whether record_memory_recall_audit is invoked at runtime at all — memory_tool_handler.py:1151 calls it, but /var/omnisight/memory has not been written since 2026-07-01, so I could not observe a live invocation.
- The exact reason the axis counter (7) and structural-half counters (5+5) disagree for the same 7 served responses; I did not root-cause it.
- The chat/specialist/a2a adapters' live tool-dispatch volume — omnisight_action_guard_decision_total has no series, which is consistent with both 'guard never invoked' and 'no tool calls in this process lifetime'.
- Whether any human currently reads /api/v1/project-state/metrics (the in-memory trace surface) — it is admin-auth'd and I did not call it.

### [CRITICAL] Prometheus has ZERO alertmanagers — every anti-hollow alert in the memory stack delivers nowhere, and the config that would fix it never reached the container

- **category:** hollow  ·  **EVIDENCE**  ·  **silent:** True
- **evidence:** curl http://localhost:9090/api/v1/alertmanagers -> {"activeAlertmanagers":[],"droppedAlertmanagers":[]}. docker ps -a | grep -i alertman -> no container has ever been created. docker inspect shows bind /home/user/omnisight-prod/configs/prometheus.yml -> /etc/prometheus/prometheus.yml; host file: inode 2402431, mtime 2026-07-22, 607 bytes, contains the OP-2563 `alerting: alertmanagers: targets: ["alertmanager:9093"]` block; container file: inode 1157526, mtime 2026-07-09, 346 bytes, `grep -n 'alerting|alertmanager'` exits 1 (no match). configs/alertmanager.yml:26 self-describes the receivers as `Name-only receiver = valid blackhole (delivers nowhere)`.
- **expected vs actual:** Expected (OP-2563 'Deploy Alertmanager + wire alerting so anti-hollow alerts page'): firing alerts fan out to a receiver. Actual: no alertmanager process exists, Prometheus has no alertmanager configured, and the single-file bind mount pinned a 2026-07-09 inode so the merged config change has been invisible for 3 weeks.
- **impact:** The entire Phase S anti-hollow alerting investment (OP-2557/2559/2560/2563) is decorative. Every finding below that 'would alert' does not. An operator inspecting the repo, the prod checkout, or the host file sees alerting correctly wired — only /api/v1/alertmanagers reveals the truth.

### [CRITICAL] OP-2558's 'fail-closed' staging-gate project-state probe has NEVER executed — 11,222 canary runs, 0 carry a project_state marker, 9,911 say green

- **category:** hollow  ·  **EVIDENCE**  ·  **silent:** True
- **evidence:** grep -c project_state /home/user/work/sora/logs/release-milestone/canary-status.jsonl -> 0 across 11,222 lines spanning 2026-05-12T03:53:31Z..2026-08-01T15:33:34Z (9,911 green / 1,307 red). Latest line: {"detail":"2 probe(s) OK on http://localhost:18080","status":"green"} with no project-state keys. Two independent causes: (a) `ls /home/user/.config/omnisight/staging-gate.env` -> No such file, so OMNISIGHT_STAGING_GATE_FIXTURE_TICKET is unset and staging_gate.py:766-772 short-circuits to health-only; (b) the unit runs `ExecStart=/usr/bin/python3 /home/user/sora-bridge/scripts/staging_gate.py` with WorkingDirectory=/home/user/sora-bridge, and `grep -c 'OP-2558\|project_state_probe' /home/user/sora-bridge/backend/agents/staging_gate.py` -> 0, on a tree 247 commits behind origin/develop.
- **expected vs actual:** Expected: 'It fails CLOSED — a missing bearer, a staging JIRA that can never be live, or a hollow/degraded half is a LOUD red canary line, never a silent pass' (staging_gate.py:120-127). Actual: the probe is opt-in behind an env var nobody set, running from a tree that does not contain the probe's code, and the gate reports green every 10 minutes.
- **impact:** The only continuously-running tripwire specifically designed to catch a hollow project-state axis has produced zero observations since it merged. staging_gate.py:736-742 explicitly documents that 'no project-state keys reach the evidence when the probe is not activated', so a consumer of canary-status.jsonl cannot distinguish 'probe passed' from 'probe never ran' — both are a bare green line.

### [HIGH] ProjectStateNoTraffic — the one alert meant to catch 'the memory injection stopped' — is broken twice: wrong metric name (permanently firing since 2026-07-09) and an expression that cannot detect a stalled counter

- **category:** bug  ·  **EVIDENCE**  ·  **silent:** True
- **evidence:** deploy/observability/prometheus/project_state_health.yml:38 -> `absent(rate(omnisight_project_state_calls_total[30m]))`. `count(omnisight_project_state_calls_total)` -> ABSENT (0 series); a repo-wide grep finds the name only in this rule and in backend/tests/test_project_state_alert_rules.py:148,213 (string-equality assertions that therefore PASS). Live: /api/v1/alerts shows state=firing, activeAt=2026-07-09T19:21:11Z, value=1e+00 — 22 days continuous. Second defect, evaluated at time=2026-07-27T12:00Z (mid-outage, zero requests served for 9 days): `absent(rate(omnisight_project_state_axis_total[30m]))` -> [] while `sum(rate(...))` -> 0. Control: the same absent() on the nonexistent name at now() -> 1.
- **expected vs actual:** Expected: fire when the runner stops requesting pickup context. Actual: fires unconditionally regardless of traffic, so it carries zero information; and had the metric name been correct, absent() would still never have fired because a counter that stops incrementing keeps producing rate()=0, not an absent series.
- **impact:** Project-state traffic did collapse — Prometheus history shows ~200-239 cumulative calls/day through 2026-07-20, then 2 calls total between 2026-07-23 and 2026-07-30. The detector for exactly that event was already stuck 'on' and, per configs/alertmanager.yml + finding #1, undeliverable anyway.

### [HIGH] ProjectStateStructuralHollow is structurally unable to fire — the BM25 lessons half returns 5 hits for any ticket key, masking a JIRA half that was useless 4 times out of 5

- **category:** hollow  ·  **EVIDENCE**  ·  **silent:** True
- **evidence:** Ran locally: `lesson_retrieval.retrieve_lessons(docs/sop/lessons, ticket_title='OP-9999', ...)` -> 5 hits, scores [0.147,0.145,0.144,0.143,0.143] (uniform noise from the shared 'OP' token); only a query with no shared tokens at all ('zzzz nonsense qqqq') returns 0. The backend image carries 101 lesson files (`docker exec ... ls /app/docs/sop/lessons | wc -l` -> 101). project_state_aggregator.py:994-1000 `_dict_has_content` skips only {ticket,jira_source,kg_source}, so a non-empty kg_neighbours alone forces content='non_empty'. Live prod: omnisight_project_state_axis_total{axis="structural",content="non_empty"} = 239 with no other content label ever recorded over 60d, while omnisight_project_state_structural_half_total shows jira useful=false 4, useful=true 1.
- **expected vs actual:** Expected (rule annotation): 'Fewer than 80% of structural project-state responses carried non-empty content ... Check JIRA and KG sources'. Actual: the structural axis is non_empty 239/239 by construction because BM25 always returns something, so the ratio is pinned at 1.0 and the alert can never cross 0.8 — even in the observed window where the JIRA half was useless on 80% of samples.
- **impact:** The flagship structural anti-hollow alert cannot detect a dead JIRA half. Worse, the kg 'useful' label is `bool(structural.get('kg_neighbours'))` (backend/api/project_state.py:129) — pure non-emptiness, not relevance — so five score-0.14 noise lessons count as a useful half and are injected into the pickup prompt as 'semantic neighbourhood'.

### [HIGH] Causal axis: every failure mode returns the exact success shape, classified 'empty', with no log on the ImportError path and no alert rule covering the axis at all

- **category:** hollow  ·  **EVIDENCE**  ·  **silent:** True
- **evidence:** backend/agents/project_state_aggregator.py:657-658 `except ImportError: return {"ticket": ticket_key, "neighbours": []}` — no log, no metric. Lines 662-669 (incident-source construction failure) and 730-737 (any query exception) return the identical dict and log at log.info, not warning. _classify_payload/_dict_has_content (982-1000) skip the 'ticket' key, so all four cases plus a genuine no-neighbours result classify as content='empty'. deploy/observability/prometheus/project_state_health.yml contains no rule with axis="causal" (or axis="temporal") — the only content rule is axis="structural". Live: omnisight_project_state_axis_total{axis="causal"} over 60d = empty:218, non_empty:20, degraded:1 (the machinery does work — negative control satisfied), and the last runner_incident was written 2026-07-20 05:40 while the causal lookback is 7 days, so today the axis is structurally incapable of returning anything.
- **expected vs actual:** Expected: an operator can distinguish 'no causal neighbours for this ticket' from 'the failure_graph import broke / the DSN is gone / the SQL errored'. Actual: all five produce {"ticket":X,"neighbours":[]} -> content='empty', with at most an INFO log line and zero alerting.
- **impact:** This is the original 2026-07-07 hollow, unrepaired, on the axis whose only writer historically never existed. If failure_graph stopped importing today, the payload, the metric, the trace, and the alert state would all be byte-identical to healthy operation.

### [HIGH] The C6 memory-recall audit trail is written only to a process-local deque(maxlen=1024) — runner_incidents has 0 MEMORY_RECALL_AUDIT rows although three query paths filter for them

- **category:** data-integrity  ·  **EVIDENCE**  ·  **silent:** True
- **evidence:** backend/agents/incident_recorder.py:160-161 `def _persist_audit(record): """Write to the durable buffer (and, when 0206 lands, to Postgres)."""` — the body only does `_buffer.append(record)` against `_buffer: Deque = deque(maxlen=_BUFFER_MAX)` (line 134-135, _BUFFER_MAX=1024). Migration 0206 HAS landed: backend/alembic/versions/0206_runner_incidents.py:46 documents 'row per recall decision, failure_class=MEMORY_RECALL_AUDIT' and :76 includes the enum value; the table exists in prod. `SELECT count(*) FROM runner_incidents WHERE failure_class='MEMORY_RECALL_AUDIT'` -> 0. Three separate SQL paths in backend/agents/failure_graph.py (list_since, get, query_by_ticket) each carry `AND failure_class != 'MEMORY_RECALL_AUDIT'`, and scripts/generate_failure_graph_fixture.py:36 does too — all filtering rows that can never exist. The caller is live at backend/agents/memory_tool_handler.py:1151.
- **expected vs actual:** Expected: a durable per-decision audit row for every cross-fleet memory recall (permitted/escalated/tier), queryable from Postgres. Actual: the rows live in a 1024-entry ring buffer that dies with the uvicorn worker; Postgres has never received one.
- **impact:** An operator auditing 'which fleet recalled whose memory, and was it permitted' queries runner_incidents, gets 0 rows, and correctly concludes nothing happened — indistinguishable from the writer having been unwired since 0206. The comment 'when 0206 lands' is a stale TODO masquerading as a completed contract.

### [HIGH] Temporal axis is hard-coded unavailable (239/239 samples), and the runner deletes the key from the prompt so the consumer cannot tell 'not wired' from 'no data'

- **category:** hollow  ·  **EVIDENCE**  ·  **silent:** True
- **evidence:** backend/agents/project_state_aggregator.py:638-641 `async def _temporal_graphiti(...): del ticket_key; return dict(TEMPORAL_UNAVAILABLE_PAYLOAD)` where the payload is {"status":"unavailable"}; line 96 emits the only operator log line ONCE at module import, not per call. Prometheus over 60d: omnisight_project_state_axis_total{axis="temporal"} has exactly one series, content="unavailable", max 239 — no non_empty sample has ever been recorded. auto-runner-jira.py:1173-1175 then strips the key entirely: `if payload.get("temporal") == {"status":"unavailable"}: payload = {k:v for k,v in payload.items() if k != "temporal"}`, and the injected preamble instructs 'Treat null or absent axes as no context'. No alert rule references axis="temporal".
- **expected vs actual:** Expected of a 'three-axis' memory: the temporal axis contributes something, or its absence is legible downstream. Actual: it is a constant, it is removed before the model sees it, and the prompt tells the model to treat that as 'no context' — the model cannot distinguish a dead axis from an empty one.
- **impact:** One third of the advertised 3D memory has been a literal constant since R5 and there is no metric threshold, alert, or prompt-visible marker that would change if it were quietly deleted. graphiti_mcp_client.py remains a 54-line wrapper whose dispatcher argument no production caller supplies.

### [MEDIUM] The action-capability kernel — the 'memory can never authorize a side effect, enforced in code' invariant — is in all-shadow mode in prod, and its telemetry counter has never been exported

- **category:** security  ·  **EVIDENCE**  ·  **silent:** True
- **evidence:** backend/agents/action_guard.py:70-72,110 — `_ENV_VAR = "OMNISIGHT_ACTION_GUARD_MODE"`; empty/unset -> empty matrix; resolve_mode() line 148 `return _MODE_MATRIX.get((adapter_namespace, family), "shadow")`. guard_tool_dispatch line 566: `proceed = mode != "enforce"` for both requires_grant and unbound_principal denials — only unknown_tool_default_deny blocks. `docker exec omnisight-productizer-backend-a-1 env` name list does NOT include OMNISIGHT_ACTION_GUARD_MODE. `count(omnisight_action_guard_decision_total)` -> ABSENT (0 series) in Prometheus and no action_guard line appears in the backend's 191-series /api/v1/metrics exposition. authorization_kernel.py module docstring: '⚠ DORMANT ... T11 flips enforcement.'
- **expected vs actual:** Expected per the U6 north star: the shared kernel's invariant is enforced in code, not prompt. Actual: in production every known mutating family observes-and-proceeds, and the labelled counter that would show mode="shadow" has never been incremented, so there is no exported signal distinguishing 'guard enforcing' from 'guard shadowing' from 'guard never invoked'.
- **impact:** The safety property the whole three-principal design rests on is currently a design document plus a dormant module. Because the decision counter has no series, an operator building a dashboard on it sees an empty panel — which reads the same as 'no violations'.

### [MEDIUM] leg-2 injection is off with zero log — the function's own docstring promises a result label on EVERY pickup, and the flag-off early return happens before any print

- **category:** mismatch-with-intent  ·  **EVIDENCE**  ·  **silent:** True
- **evidence:** auto-runner-jira.py:1081-1086: docstring says 'result label logged on EVERY pickup (audit #6: hollow serving must be visible from runner logs)', then `if os.environ.get("OMNISIGHT_RUNNER_LEARNED_ITEMS","").strip().lower() not in (...): return ""` with no print. `cut -d= -f1 /home/user/.config/omnisight/runner.env` shows 10 variable names, none of them OMNISIGHT_RUNNER_LEARNED_ITEMS; the runner-claude@ unit and its five drop-ins do not set it either. Prod DB: learned_item_versions=0, learned_item_snapshots=0, learned_item_citations=0, learned_item_utility=0. `count(omnisight_memory_delivery_total)` -> ABSENT (0 series). Same shape at auto-runner-jira.py:1246-1247 for project-state: flag-off returns "" with no log.
- **expected vs actual:** Expected: hollow serving is visible from runner logs on every pickup. Actual: the single most likely hollow state (flag never set) is the one state that logs nothing at all.
- **impact:** The worker experience loop's terminal step — injection — is off and produces no evidence of being off. A reviewer reading runner logs sees no learned_items line and cannot tell whether the feature ran and found nothing, failed, or was never enabled.

### [MEDIUM] memory_enabled gauge is designated as the disambiguator between two kill switches but is only set inside a function nothing calls — it reads 0.0 for 'disabled' and for 'never ran' alike

- **category:** hollow  ·  **EVIDENCE**  ·  **silent:** True
- **evidence:** backend/learned_item_loader.py:47-52 comments: 'which switch is off is disambiguated by the memory_enabled gauge (publish switch) + logs'. The only assignment in the whole repo outside tests is learned_item_loader.py:106 `metrics.memory_enabled.set(1 if _promotion_enabled() else 0)`, inside `async def refresh_scope`, which the module docstring says is 'CALLER-DRIVEN ... no timers, no threads, no task-spawning here'. Live exposition: `omnisight_memory_enabled 0.0` — the prometheus_client default for a never-set Gauge. Sibling gauge omnisight_memory_liveness_heartbeat 0.0 has NO writer anywhere outside backend/metrics.py and tests (verified by repo-wide grep).
- **expected vs actual:** Expected: gauge=0 means the publish kill switch is off. Actual: gauge=0 also means refresh_scope has never executed, which is the actual state in prod, so the designated disambiguator cannot disambiguate.
- **impact:** Any runbook step of the form 'check memory_enabled to see which switch is off' returns a false-confident answer. memory_liveness_heartbeat is a fully dead gauge that will read 0 forever — an alert on its staleness would either never fire or always fire depending on which direction it was written.

### [MEDIUM] Anti-hollow monitors were built and merged but never installed as systemd units — including the incident-write canary for the exact stream that died on 2026-07-20

- **category:** hollow  ·  **EVIDENCE**  ·  **silent:** True
- **evidence:** Diffing deploy/systemd/ against ~/.config/systemd/user/: NOT-INSTALLED incident-write-canary.service, incident-write-canary.timer (OP-2544), memory-tool-monitor.service/.timer, memory-tool-storage-monitor.service/.timer, cognee-drift-detect.service/.timer, cognee-nightly-rebuild.service/.timer, staging-gate-smoke.timer. The omnisight-prefixed copies that DO exist: `systemctl --user is-enabled omnisight-cognee-nightly-rebuild.timer` -> disabled, `is-active` -> inactive, `show -p LastTriggerUSec` -> empty (never triggered); same for omnisight-cognee-drift-detect.timer. Neither appears in `systemctl --user list-timers --all` (24 timers listed). `systemctl --user show -p Result omnisight-cognee-nightly-rebuild.service` -> Result=success with an empty ExecMainExitTimestamp — systemd reports 'success' for a unit that has never run. Prod DB confirms the gap mattered: runner_incidents max(created_at) = 2026-07-20 05:40:06, i.e. incident writes stopped 12 days ago with no canary to notice.
- **expected vs actual:** Expected: OP-2544's daily read-only canary escalates to JIRA when the incident write rate drops; OP-2551 'Revive cognee nightly rebuild unit'. Actual: neither timer exists or has ever fired, and `systemctl show -p Result` returns success for never-started units, so a status sweep reads clean.
- **impact:** The causal axis's only data source went silent for 12 days with zero notification. The reviving of cognee shipped a unit file and stopped there. `systemctl --user show -p Result` is itself a success-reporting-nothing surface for never-started oneshots.

### [MEDIUM] memory_tool_monitor's only data source does not exist on the host

- **category:** hollow  ·  **EVIDENCE**  ·  **silent:** True
- **evidence:** scripts/memory_tool_monitor.py:21 `DEFAULT_METRICS = DEFAULT_ROOT / "metrics.jsonl"` with DEFAULT_ROOT=/var/omnisight/memory. `ls -la /var/omnisight/memory/metrics.jsonl` -> No such file or directory. The directory itself exists with per-fleet subdirs whose newest mtime is 2026-07-01 (default/), most 2026-05-24..26. The monitor's unit is also not installed (previous finding).
- **expected vs actual:** Expected: eviction/cap-pressure monitoring over the Claude memory-tool store with WARN at 70% and PAGE at 90%. Actual: no metrics file has ever been produced, and no process would read it if it were.
- **impact:** Memory-tool storage pressure (the OP-908 cap-proposal loop) is unobserved end-to-end: no producer, no consumer, no unit. If eviction started silently discarding fleet memory, nothing would record it.

### [MEDIUM] 73% of runner_incidents carry failure_class='OTHER', so the causal axis's same_failure_class edges are noise that is indistinguishable from signal

- **category:** design  ·  **EVIDENCE**  ·  **silent:** True
- **evidence:** `SELECT failure_class, count(*) FROM runner_incidents GROUP BY 1` -> OTHER 1448, BRIDGE_DESYNC 464, UNKNOWN_AREA_LABEL 26, MERGE_CONFLICT 23, RUNNER_TIMEOUT 6, WORKTREE_DIRTY 3, LLM_LOOP_DETECTED 3 (total 1973). backend/agents/failure_graph.py:27-29 defines same_failure_class as one of three edge kinds; project_state_aggregator.py:706-724 takes the first 3 edges per own-incident with no score or relevance filter and ships them as 'neighbours'.
- **expected vs actual:** Expected: 'incidents X and Y share a failure_class cluster' captures a fleet-wide burst of the same failure mode. Actual: for 73% of rows the shared class is the catch-all, so any two OTHER incidents in the 7-day window are edge-connected and the top-3 neighbours are effectively arbitrary.
- **impact:** The causal axis can report non_empty (as it did 20 times) while the content is meaningless. Combined with the empty/degraded conflation above, 'causal: non_empty' is not evidence the axis is working, and there is no metric that distinguishes a signal neighbour from an OTHER-OTHER pairing.

### [LOW] Two Prometheus rule files are mounted into the rules directory but are not listed in rule_files: — they are never evaluated

- **category:** bug  ·  **EVIDENCE**  ·  **silent:** True
- **evidence:** `docker exec omnisight-productizer-prometheus-1 ls /etc/prometheus/obs-rules/` -> alerts.yml (5715 bytes), frontend-freshness-alerts.yml (1010 bytes), project_state_health.yml. The live prometheus.yml `rule_files:` lists only project_state_health.yml. `curl /api/v1/rules` returns exactly one group: omnisight_project_state_health with 4 rules. deploy/observability/prometheus/alerts.yml:20-21 self-documents 'Load into Prometheus by adding this file to the top-level rule_files: list' — never done.
- **expected vs actual:** Expected: the HA-07 replica-lag / 5xx-rate / instance-down alerts (G7 #3) are active. Actual: the file is mounted, readable, and syntactically fine, but Prometheus never parses it.
- **impact:** Mounting a rule file makes it look deployed to anyone who inspects the container filesystem; only /api/v1/rules reveals that one group of four rules is the entire alerting surface of this Prometheus.

### [LOW] backend/metrics.py degrades the entire metric surface to _NoOp() on a prometheus_client ImportError

- **category:** hollow  ·  **EVIDENCE**  ·  **silent:** True
- **evidence:** backend/metrics.py:35 `try:` / :45 `except ImportError:  # pragma: no cover` -> at :1254 `class _NoOp:` and ~130 assignments including project_state_axis_total, project_state_structural_half_total, project_state_axis_latency_seconds (1375-1377), action_guard_* (1391-1393), memory_* (1388-1389), u6_l2_* (1400). Not currently triggered: the live exposition carries 191 omnisight_ series.
- **expected vs actual:** Expected: a missing metrics dependency is loud. Actual: it silently converts every anti-hollow counter, gauge and histogram into a no-op while the app serves 200s normally.
- **impact:** A single dependency-resolution change in the backend image would blank the whole observability surface with no error. Because every alert in finding #3/#4 is ratio- or absent-based over these metrics, the resulting state reads as 'inactive/healthy', not 'no data'. Inference: I did not force this branch, so the blast radius is reasoned from the code, not observed.

### [LOW] Runner-side project-state injection logs nothing when the feature flag is off — flag-off is indistinguishable from a successful injection in runner logs

- **category:** hollow  ·  **EVIDENCE**  ·  **silent:** True
- **evidence:** auto-runner-jira.py:1246-1247 `if not agent_feature_flags.is_project_state_inject_enabled_sync(): return ""` — no print. The sibling paths do log: skipped_by_label (1249-1253), fetch_failed (1142-1146), malformed_response (1150-1160). agent_feature_flags.py:47-51 sets project_state default=False, so an unset OMNISIGHT_PROJECT_STATE_INJECT silently disables injection. Currently the flag IS set to true in /home/user/.config/omnisight/runner.env, so the path is live today.
- **expected vs actual:** Expected: parity with the other three degrade paths, which all emit a marker line. Actual: the default-off state is the only one that leaves no trace.
- **impact:** If the env file were rotated, replaced, or the EnvironmentFile drop-in removed, every pickup would silently lose its memory block and the runner log would be byte-identical to a healthy run. This is a low-severity instance of the same pattern, listed because it is the failure mode most likely to recur during an env rotation.
